# Skills · G · part 10

852 entries, garmin-integration to gate-exchange-alpha. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **garmin-integration**: Garmin Connect OAuth integration for the RunSmart PWA. Covers the complete OAuth 2.0 flow, token storage in Dexie.js (IndexedDB), activity fetching, device lifecycle (connect/sync/disconnect), and the thin-backend architecture pattern. Use when working on Garmin connection features, debugging OAuth issues, adding Garmin data, or extending the wearable device system. ([nadavyigal/Running-coach-](https://github.com/nadavyigal/Running-coach-/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/garmin-integration) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **garmin-mcp**: Connect an MCP-compatible agent to local Garmin Connect sleep, Body Battery, HRV, stress, activities, and training readiness. Use when an AI agent needs setup, usage, safety boundaries, or troubleshooting for Garmin MCP. ([Lord1Egypt/awesome-skill-forge](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/awesome-skill-forge/tree/HEAD/community/clawhub/g/garmin-mcp) · ★3)
- **garmin-panorama-training-analysis**: 基于佳明（Garmin Connect）数据的全景训练分析。拉取活动/逐公里明细(lapDTOs)/每日恢复(HRV·RHR·睡眠·Body Battery)/负荷平衡(ACWR·Form·就绪度)，融合 Open-Meteo 天气，输出 Ride Relief 暗黑风格离线 HTML 报告。能力：逐公里技术分解(配速/心率/步频/步幅/垂直振幅/触地时间)、心率5区训练比例、热适应评价(WBGT/露点/湿球/酷热指数)、训练状态与成绩预测(Riegel)、身体成分与能量。触发词：佳明分析、Garmin 训练报告、跑步分析、周报月报、热适应、LSD分析、负荷分析、ACWR、就绪度、训练状态。 ([runzhenghengbin/garmin-panorama-training-analysis](https://github.com/runzhenghengbin/garmin-panorama-training-analysis/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
